## Cyprus added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cyprus's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **75 distinct works: 22 pupil textbooks (primary Grades 1–6, part-books) and 53 teacher guides (Οδηγός Εκπαιδευτικού), all Cyprus Ministry of Education (Υπουργείο Παιδείας), in Greek** — and placed 281 teaching items: 228 from the pupil books and 53 teacher-evidence items (one per guide, worded from the guide's stated success indicators, ΔΕΙΚΤΕΣ ΕΠΙΤΥΧΙΑΣ). SET CORRECTION: the expansion map recorded Cyprus Maths as 22 student + 43 teacher = 65 in hand; the records' union proves 22 student + 53 teacher = **75** (staging books.json carries 64 files = 22 student + 42 teacher, all secured in GitHub; textbooks.tsv adds 11 further teacher guides held as git-blob files that are in no books.json: Grade 1 Units 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 9, 10, 11, Grade 2 Unit 1, Grade 4 Unit 1) — the correction is recorded here; the library records themselves are not edited (merges never edit the records). In the sections below the pupil books are cited as Books S1–S22 and the guides as Books T1–T53, in grade order. Cyprus COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its pupil-book rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states the pupil book's unit, its indicators and its lesson organisation — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). The read's three flagged claims are carried exactly: (1) the map's “65” is corrected above; (2) the Grade 3 Unit 1 teacher guide's indicator Αρ2.1 states quantities “to 10,000” where its pupil book's Unit 1 teaches to 1,000 — the guide's figure is NOT confirmed by the pupil book and is worded as the guide's own claim in that guide's row, never as pupil teaching; (3) three guides carry the books' own unit-number mislabels — the Grade 4 Unit 3 guide's contents header reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 2”, the Grade 6 Unit 3 guide's running head reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 2”, the Grade 6 Unit 9 guide's running head reads “ΕΝΟΤΗΤΑ 8” — the contents match their cover units, so the rows record the true units with the errors noted in the book entries, taught corrected, never as printed. Production date stamps inside three files (Grade 5 Part 1 “22/06/2026”, Grade 6 Part 1's InDesign stamp 05/03/2026, Grade 6 Part 3 “01/07/2026”) are PDF production stamps in the ministry files as held, not edition dates. The placements' named exclusions — front matter, icon keys, contents pages, cut-out card appendices (“Για τη σελίδα …”), Διαμορφωτική Αξιολόγηση (formative assessment) pages and enrichment sets repeating unit content — are stocked nowhere; the apparent “330C/20C” in the Grade 3 Part 1 enrichment is the degree sign merging with the digits in the text layer (33 °C falling by 2 °C), not a book error. Teacher-guide coverage in hand: Grades 1–3 Units 1–12 complete, Grade 4 Units 1–3 only, Grade 5 Units 1–4, Grade 6 Units 1–10; the further guides that exist only as ministry hub links (Grade 4 Units 4+, Grade 5 Units 5+, Grade 6 Units 11+) are not in hand, not read and not counted. Cyprus's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (75/75) has been read and placed; if the hub-link guides are later secured, Cyprus reverts to PARTIAL until they are read.

Into this lesson the placements stock 28 items (22 from pupil books, 6 teacher evidence) from Book S2, Book S3, Book S4, Book S5, Book S7, Book S8, Book S10, Book S16, Book S18, Book T6, Book T9, Book T13, Book T19, Book T26, Book T31.

## Sri Lanka added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Sri Lanka's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs in two passes — **94 files / 66 distinct works: 26 pupil textbooks and 40 teacher guides, all Sri Lanka Educational Publications Department (EPD) / National Institute of Education (NIE), in the country's three media — English, Sinhala and Tamil** — and placed 479 items: 289 from the pupil books and 190 teacher-evidence items (the secondary guides at one item per term of the guide's own lesson sequence; the primary guides at one item per syllabus competency group or per part). Pass 1 read the expansion map's 59 files (32 works); Pass 2 pinned 35 further in-hand files the map's subject filter had missed — 34 NIE teacher guides for Grades 1–13 and one pupil textbook (Book S26, filed under the subject "Science for Technology") — and read every one. In the sections below the pupil books are cited as Books S1–S26 and the guides as Books T1–T40. Sri Lanka COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure only on the strength of its pupil-book rows; its guide rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — a guide states its syllabus's competencies, its lesson sequence and its teaching instructions — cited as the evidence kind they are and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Set facts carried exactly: (1) the Grade 9 Sinhala pupil book is held only as chapter files of two different printings (Books S9–S10) whose shared chapter 20 is a different chapter in each, and NO chapter-26 file exists in either split — the English twin's chapter 26 is Algebraic fractions; the hole is carried as a set gap, not as read; (2) the 2018 Sinhala Grade 9 teacher guide is filed twice (two scans of one work) and is placed once (Book T2), and the file held as the Grade 7 2008 manual's "Tamil" copy is byte-identical to the English manual (same git blob SHA) and is cited once, as the English manual (Book T10); (3) the year-less Tamil Grade 4 and Grade 5 guides are proved by byte size to be NIE's 2015 editions, not the far larger 2019/2020 scans NIE also lists (Books T39–T40); (4) the 1976 Grade 12 Mathematics Part I scan (Book S23) ends with some six pages of an unrelated German textbook's verb tables (PDF pp. 271–276) — its mathematics runs to PDF p. 270 and its last chapters were sampled, not contents-verified; (5) the Grade 1 Sinhala 2026 guide is in hand as a Term 1 volume only (Book T33) — its Terms 2–3 are stocked nowhere — and the Grade 1 Tamil 2016 guide (Book T31) is marked DRAFT on its cover and is placed as filed; (6) the Grade 11 guide's Term 2 table labels one lesson (competency 23.6, inside the geometry cluster) "Central limit theorem" — recorded as printed in Book T19's entry; no row in this course rests on that label; (7) Grade 10 Mathematics Part I (English) and Grade 7 Mathematics Part I (Tamil, 2007–2015 edition) are NOT in hand in any filing — library gaps, not unread books; their twins in the other medium are held and read (Books S17 and S13/S14). The placements' named exclusions — revision exercises, answers, glossaries, assessment apparatus, and the guides' specimen lesson plans and school-based assessment instruments beyond their sequences — are stocked nowhere. Sri Lanka's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (94/94 files) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

The filed copy is marked DRAFT on its cover; it is placed as filed, and the mark is recorded here.

## Colombia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Colombia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **27 distinct works: 26 student books and 1 teacher guide, all Colombia Ministerio de Educación Nacional (MEN), in Spanish** — Escuela Nueva Matemáticas cartillas Grades 1–5 (11), Nivelemos Matemáticas student workbooks Grades 1–4 (4), Aulas sin fronteras Grade 6 Unidad 1 student book and its teacher guide (2), Postprimaria student guides Grades 6–9 (4), Secundaria Activa student modules Grades 6–9 (4), and Vamos a Aprender Matemáticas 10 and 11 (2) — 3,928 PDF pages — and placed 293 teaching items: 288 from the student books and 5 teacher-evidence items from the one teacher guide (Book 23). SET CORRECTIONS carried exactly: (1) the expansion map's “28 student + 1 teacher = 29” over-counts by 2 — textbooks.tsv carries duplicate records of Escuela Nueva Grado 1 Cartilla 1 and Grado 2 Cartilla 1 (old blob/main rows for the same two works that also landed as release assets); the distinct set is 27 works and the duplicates are cited once (the library records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records); (2) the set in hand contains NO Grade 3 Cartilla 3 — Grade 3 holds Cartillas 1–2 plus the Nivelemos Grade 3 workbook, and Cartilla 2 completes Unidades 6–9; nothing in hand is a mislabelled Cartilla 3, and no never-published ruling is needed or made; (3) Nivelemos Matemáticas Grado 1 (Book 10) has NO asset in any GitHub release — it was read from the MEN source file itself, landed at exactly the census size (5,020,415 bytes) with EOF intact; the library side still owes that file a GitHub landing. The two Vamos a Aprender books (Books 26–27) are image-only viewer assemblies with no text layer, read from rendered pages (covers, printed contents pp. 6–7 and body spot-checks — Matemáticas 10 printed p. 110 “Teorema del seno”, Matemáticas 11 printed p. 120 “Derivada de una función en un punto”); printed page = PDF page in the sampled range. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–27 in the placements' order. Colombia COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its student-book rows; Book 23's rows are TEACHER EVIDENCE — the guide companions Book 15 with teaching plans, activity scripts and answers and teaches no student content itself — cited as the evidence kind it is and never the basis of the count (the standing Ghana evidence precedent for guide rows). Book errors: none material at placement grain — the Secundaria Activa Grade 9 module's contents page misprints two of its own headings (“Captítulo”, “Undiad 2”); the content is unaffected and rows use the corrected unit names. The placements' named exclusions — presentation letters, “how this book works” pages, author/imprint pages, transversal civics/health/environment themes, practice/evaluation apparatus (“Practica más”, “Evaluación del aprendizaje”, answer keys) and the Escuela Nueva “Estándares/Competencias” boxes at each unidad opening (MEN's own curriculum-standard statements; the taught content is placed at the guías themselves) — are stocked nowhere. The Círculos de Aprendizaje integrated books and the Retos para Gigantes Transición weekly guides are NOT in this set (filed as integrado, not matematicas) and are stocked nowhere. Colombia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (27/27) has been read and placed, on the in-hand definition.

## Andorra added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Andorra's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them official Escola Andorrana curriculum programmes published by decree in the Butlletí Oficial del Principat d'Andorra (BOPA); 0 pupil (student) books** — and placed 679 curriculum-requirement items. A programme states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Andorra item in this section is therefore TEACHER EVIDENCE — a curriculum requirement, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is; Andorra is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until Andorran pupil books are in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded 3 books in hand; the true set is 4 — the grades 1–6 programme (Decret 376/2021, Annex VII) is held in the GitHub repo but sits in no staging books.json, which is why the map missed it. All four programmes are Andorra's OWN (Govern d'Andorra, in Catalan); the Spanish and French systems also taught in Andorra (Spain's Anaya and Innovamat manuals; France's Le Livre Scolaire) are list-only names on official school book lists — no file is held, so they are cited nowhere in this course and credited to no country — and the Escola Andorrana itself has no commercial pupil-textbook series, so programmes are all that exist to hold. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct stated requirement — each specific competency, and each distinct entry in the programmes' learning-resource tables, tagged by cycle where the table splits by cycle — and a procedure entry that merely restates a concept entry is folded into it, not double-counted. The placements' named exclusions — the decree preambles, the introductions and competency rationale, the didactic orientations, the assessment criteria and end-of-cycle expectations, the synthesis tables (verified verbatim repeats of the competency sections), the attitudes/values lists, Book 1's glossary and opening summary table, and the Council of Europe cross-references — are stocked nowhere. Book 3's synthesis table words its C2 end-of-cycle expectation as modelling "situacions cientificotècniques" (scientific-technical) inside the social-sciences programme — a copy-over slip in the decree as published; Book 3's competency section itself is consistently social-sciences, and the slip is copied into no row below. Book 2 states its cone/polyhedron sections twice (forms table and models table, printed pp. 32–33) — counted once in the placements and stocked once. Printed page = PDF page in all four books (Books 2–4 carry the BOPA folio; Book 1's Portal PDF is paginated at the foot the same way). Into this lesson the placements stock 10 teacher-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Programa de matemàtiques de primera ensenyança* (curriculum programme, Grades 1–6) — Andorra, Govern d'Andorra, Escola Andorrana; Decret 376/2021, Annex VII; ISBN 978-99920-0-934-5; 688,455 bytes, 49 PDF pages; printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — an official curriculum programme: it states what is required to be taught and teaches nothing itself.

## Angola added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Angola's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 books, all pupil textbooks: Matemática, Manuais da 1.ª–6.ª Classe (Ministério da Educação — MED, Angola; updated editions of 2021, in Portuguese). There is no teacher guide in the Angola Maths set in hand, so every Angola row in this course is a pupil-textbook row — there are no teacher-evidence rows, and Angola counts in every lesson it stocks** — and placed 229 teaching items. The expansion map's “6 student + 0 teacher = 6” is correct: download-lists/angola.md, the phase3-staging checklist, textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json (“pushed”) and the staging books.json files all give the same six distinct pupil textbooks, and nothing else. None of the six PDFs was on local disk (pusher-secured only), so all six were fetched fresh for the read from the books' recorded second official source, the ministry's Xilonga host, byte-exact against the records. One set nuance: the portal's 32-manual set contains no 2.ª Classe Matemática at all — the 2.ª book in hand (Book 2) exists only as an unlinked Xilonga file (MAT02-LR.pdf) found by pattern probe on 2026-10-23. A Guia Prático Matemática 6ª exists only as an exact-link reader copy and is NOT in hand — cited nowhere; if one is ever secured it enters as teacher evidence only. The Book 2 and Book 4 PDFs carry a massively duplicated text layer (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the files; the content is fully legible and both books were read in full from it. Twelve book errors are recorded in the placements; they are carried in the book entries below and taught corrected. The placements' named exclusions — the apresentação (ministerial foreword letter) and anthem/flag/civic front matter, the bibliografia pages, pure colouring, tracing and copying practice with no new concept, the editorial production notes printed inside the books, and exercise sets as such (exercises practise the item under which they sit) — are stocked nowhere. Printed-page bases: Books 1 and 3 — printed page = PDF page − 1; Books 2, 4, 5 and 6 — printed page = PDF page. Into this lesson the placements stock 10 items (all from pupil textbooks) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4 and Book 5.

**Book 1:** *Matemática — Manual da 1.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 1) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Editora Moderna, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-762-267-0; 4,293,873 bytes, 81 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1).

**Book 2:** *Matemática — Manual da 2.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 2) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Mayamba Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-761-297-8; 3,437,647 bytes, 56 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book error, taught corrected: printed p. 23 calls the minuend “diminuendo ou aditivo” — “aditivo” is wrong for subtraction; the course teaches minuend. Editorial production notes are printed as lesson content on printed p. 50 — production matter, cited nowhere. The PDF's text layer is massively duplicated (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the file; the content is fully legible and the book was read in full from it.

**Book 3:** *Matemática — Manual da 3.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 3) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Progresso Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-762-270-0; 1,798,728 bytes, 85 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page − 1). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 21 repeats the minuend misnomer “diminuendo ou aditivo” (teach: minuend); printed p. 36's decimal-multiplication worked example is internally inconsistent — it states 4,15 × 2,3, lays out 3,15 and prints 7,245 (correct: 4,15 × 2,3 = 9,545); the exercise under “Noção de circunferência” at printed p. 44 repeats the parallel-lines exercise verbatim (a copy-paste error in the book); printed p. 62 prints the false line “20 m = 200 cm” (correct: 20 m = 2 000 cm); printed p. 64's formula under decâmetro repeats “1 hm = 100 m” (correct: 1 dam = 10 m).

**Book 4:** *Matemática — Manual da 4.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 4) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Mayamba Editora, 2021, 1.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-761-296-1; 1,983,954 bytes, 112 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book error, taught corrected: printed p. 43 claims division “is only possible if the dividend is a multiple of the divisor” (exact division only) — contradicted by Book 3's own division-with-remainder teaching; the course teaches division with remainder. Editorial production notes are printed as page content on printed pp. 17, 18, 21, 24, 25, 28, 30 and 45 — production matter, cited nowhere; the Tema 3 heading is misprinted inside the book (at printed p. 102); PDF pp. 106–112 are blank/back matter apart from the bibliografia (printed p. 107), cited nowhere. The PDF's text layer is massively duplicated (the same text overprinted many times per page) — a production flaw of the file; the content is fully legible and the book was read in full from it.

**Book 5:** *Matemática — Manual da 5.ª Classe* (pupil textbook, Grade 5) — Angola, Ministério da Educação (MED), Texto Editores, 2021, 2.ª Edição, ISBN 978-989-8884-84-8; 4,730,235 bytes, 128 PDF pp. Pages cited are the book's printed pages (printed page = PDF page). Book errors, taught corrected: printed p. 29's worked example 12 + 37 + 7 + 3 is printed as = 55 with a garbled regrouping (correct total: 59); printed p. 73's equivalence line prints 1/3 = 2/6 = 2/9 (the last term should be 3/9); printed p. 127's median worked example uses an ordered list containing 12, which is not in the exercise's data, and drops one 10 (the median is taught from the data as printed in the exercise: ordered 6, 7, 9, 10, 10, 10, 11, 13, 14 → median 10).

## Bahamas added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Bahamas' whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **4 documents, all of them Bahamas Ministry of Education curriculum or examination-syllabus documents; 0 pupil (student) textbooks and 0 teacher guides** — and placed 496 requirement items. A curriculum states what is REQUIRED to be taught; it teaches nothing itself. Every Bahamas item in this section is therefore CURRICULUM EVIDENCE — a statement of what the Bahamas requires, worded as a requirement — cited as the evidence kind it is (Book 3's items are examination-SYLLABUS evidence, on the same standing); the Bahamas is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana/Barbados evidence-only precedent, until a Bahamas pupil book is in hand and read. Set correction, as the placements prove it: the expansion map recorded the in-hand set as “4 student + 0 teacher” — the count of 4 is right, the kind is wrong; all four documents are ministry requirement documents (kind = curriculum in both the staging books.json files and textbooks.tsv). The file the records title *BJC Mathematics Curriculum, Grades 7–9* is actually the *Bahamas Junior Certificate 044 Mathematics Syllabus 2003* — a 10-page scanned examination syllabus with no text layer, read visually page by page — and the held Senior High file is the July 2019 revision of the Grades 10–12 guidelines; both are placed as what they are. No pupil textbook or teacher guide is in hand: the pupil books Bahamas schools use are commercial (Hodder *Bahamas Primary Mathematics* Books 1–6 — existence verified in the records, no file held; Books 1–2 name Silver Burdett Ginn Mathematics and Harcourt Math in classroom use, and Book 4's bibliography names STP Caribbean Mathematics, Mathematics: A Complete Course, Fundamental Mathematics for the Caribbean and Nelson Caribbean Mathematics — named only, none held), and the one school-compiled pupil text with an exact link in the records (Akhepran International Academy, *Mathematics BJC Textbook — Grade 7*, 2023) was never downloaded and sits in no record set, so it is not part of this read and is cited nowhere. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct numbered objective in each guidelines book's Scope of Work body — the Content column read with every objective — Book 3 at one item per numbered topic row of its Topics table and Book 4 at one item per CONCEPTS/TOPICS block; an objective restated in full on a continuation page is counted once, at its first full statement, and the two requirements the documents restate inside a second strand (Book 1 Grade 2 estimation, item 69; Book 2 Grade 5 rounding of decimals, item 256) are stocked as the placements place them, each row naming the restatement. The placements' named exclusions — Benchmarks, Scope-and-Sequence charts, pacing guides and Essential Questions in Books 1–2; the Problems of the Day sets, Materials Lists, assessment rubrics, the Children's Literature list and References (record items R1–R7 and R9–R11); Book 3's examination administration, its 19 general aims and its erratum sheet (R8); Book 4's Scope and Sequence charts, Glossary and Technology Integration pages — are stocked nowhere. Book errors found in the read are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 19 requirement-evidence items from Book 1 and Book 2.

CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — a curriculum states what is required to be taught; it teaches nothing itself.

## Belize added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Belize's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 documents, all of them Belize Ministry of Education *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide* books (August 2012) — Infant 1, Standard 1 and Standard IV; 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 108 teaching items. Every document in the set is a teacher's guide: it states the learning outcomes pupils must reach and teaches the teacher how to teach each one (comments, sample lesson objectives, specific content, sample activities and assessment). Every Belize item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — Belize's guides teach teachers how to teach the topic; no Belize pupil textbook is in hand — cited as the evidence kind it is; Belize is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Belize pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the pupil books on the official National Textbook Program list (*My BZ Math* Infant 1–Standard 4, Macmillan; *Caribbean Primary Math* Bk 6 for Standard 5, Hodder; *Active Math* for Standard 6, Macmillan) are all recorded missing in the library files, and the guides' own textbook linkages point to the legacy pupil series *Primary Mathematics for Belize* (Hodder, by term codes Y1T1…/S1T1…), which is named by the guides only — not in hand, not read, and not a course source. Three further Maths documents — the *Primary curriculum: Mathematics* division webs (Lower Division, Middle Division, Upper Division) — are recorded at exact link only, never downloaded and in no books.json; they are not part of this read and are cited nowhere. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct numbered learning outcome in each guide's Standards and Learning Outcomes section (35 outcomes in each guide, 105 in all) plus 3 items taught in a detailed section but stated in no outcome of that guide (items 64, 65 and 87, marked D); outcome numbering is continuous across the series (Infant 1 carries M1.1–M20.1, Standard 1 continues at M1.10, Standard IV at M1.16), which is why the codes do not restart at 1 in the later guides. Printed page = PDF page in all three guides. The placements' named exclusions — the guides' front matter (programme philosophy: goals, rationale, assessment discussion), strand introductions and Big Ideas prose, sample lesson objectives, sample assessments, sample activities and print/technology resource columns as apparatus, the shared manipulatives list, the websites lists, the shared Mathematics Glossary (carried in the placements as record item R2 — a teacher reference list, cited nowhere as content), and the standards each guide marks N/A at its level (Book 1: M2 place value and M13 equations; Book 2: M14 measurement concepts; Book 3: M5 position/ranking numbers and M14) — are stocked nowhere. The 14 book errors and inconsistencies found in the read (placements E1–E14) are carried corrected on the rows they touch: item 35 is placed as data interpretation, not probability, though the guide files it under its probability standard (E5); item 51's set language is taught with “complement” for the book's “compliment” (E6); item 66's many-to-one correspondence is taught as one symbol or unit standing for many objects, not the guide's function-style definition (E8); item 82 is stocked WITHOUT the outcome's fourth bullet (“expand powers with same base to add or subtract”), which is not a valid add/subtract procedure (E9); item 81 is placed as stated and flagged as vague and advanced for its level (E10); item 104 is placed as measurement and taught with the standard measurement meanings, the guide's own definition of precision being a data-management sense (E13); and item 106 follows the guide's own correct definitions — its assessment text's “medium” for median is an error (E12). Into this lesson the placements stock 6 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2 and Book 3.

**Book 1:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Infant 1* (teacher guide, Grade 1) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 738,164 bytes, 58 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–16; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 17–52. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Standard 1* (teacher guide, Grade 3) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 803,827 bytes, 62 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–16; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 17–56. Printed page = PDF page. Outcome numbering continues the series from Book 1 (M1.10 onwards). TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

**Book 3:** *Mathematics Curriculum: Teacher's Guide, Standard IV* (teacher guide, Grade 6) — Belize, Ministry of Education, August 2012; 868,323 bytes, 51 PDF pages. Standards and Learning Outcomes section printed pp. 12–15; detailed teaching sections printed pp. 16–45. Printed page = PDF page. Outcome numbering continues the series (M1.16 onwards). The detailed sections of this guide are markedly thinner than in Books 1–2 (several state “Specific Content: N/A”); placements rest on the outcomes as stated. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

## Australia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Australia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 books, all of them student textbooks** (Years 8–10: eight Cambridge volumes — the Essential Mathematics Australian Curriculum, Victorian Curriculum and CambridgeMATHS NSW lines, plus the GOLD support edition and the CORE pathway volume — and one Jacaranda volume, Maths Quest 10+10A) — and placed 844 teaching items. Australia has no government textbook series (ACARA writes the Australian Curriculum but publishes and prescribes no textbooks); the set is the school-used commercial series the country records name, and every book is cited as such. All 9 files are distinct works (distinct ISBNs, extents and contents, verified per book on the read); the publisher-line relations are stated in the placements: Book A1 (GOLD) is the support edition of Book A2's Year 8 material, Book A3 is the same line in its Victorian ©2024 edition, Books A4 and A6 are the NSW syllabus editions, and Book A9 is Book A8's CORE pathway companion. In the sections below the books are cited as Books A1–A9. Australia COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every item is pupil-textbook teaching. The read's four flagged claims are carried exactly: (F1) Book A3's contents lists its Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the file in hand contains the full chapter, bound after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941) — it is read and placed, and it founds this course's algorithmic-thinking lesson; (F2) Book A7's contents lists a Chapter 18, Algorithmic thinking, with no page numbers, and the chapter is NOT in the file (searched end to end; the book closes with its Glossary, printed p. 1106, and Index, printed p. 1117) — recorded as absent, not read, and no item rests on it; (F3) Book A9's running head at printed p. 644 prints section 10E's code as “0E” — the book's own production error; the placement cites 10E, as the section's heading and contents give it; (F4) only the 9 Maths books are this read's set — the country records' wider Australia picture (12 staged books across subjects, plus filled-by-exact-link titles) is not merged into this count, and in particular the two senior Maths titles that exist only as exact-link records (Cambridge Senior Specialist Mathematics, QCE Units 3&4, 2e, and Cambridge Senior Mathematical Methods, QCE Units 3&4, 2e) are NOT in hand and were NOT read. The placements' named exclusions — front matter and “how to use this book” guides; every chapter's pre-tests, progress quizzes, unlettered applications / problem-solving / modelling / investigation spreads, puzzles and games, chapter summaries, checklists and reviews, semester reviews, glossaries, indexes and answer sections — are apparatus/assessment, teach no new content, and are stocked nowhere. The set is Years 8–10 secondary only, so the primary lessons (counting, place value, first steps) receive no Australia item — that is the set's shape, not a reading gap. Australia's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand book (9/9) has been read and placed; the two senior QCE exact-link titles do not block the in-hand class (if exact-link titles are ever ruled in-scope for the courses reckoning, Australia drops to PARTIAL until they are grabbed and read). Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items from Book A1, Book A2, Book A3.

**Book A1:** *Cambridge Mathematics 8 (CambridgeMaths GOLD)* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2012, ISBN 978-1-107-64383-3; 62,774,078 bytes, 554 PDF pp. The GOLD support edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line, developed for students who require additional support in mathematics; a distinct work from Book A2 (different ISBN, extent and arrangement), not a duplicate file. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 14).

**Book A2:** *Essential Mathematics for the Australian Curriculum Year 8, Third Edition* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2019, ISBN 978-1-108-77281-5; 35,833,730 bytes, 846 PDF pp. The main Year 8 Australian Curriculum volume of the Essential Mathematics line (Book A1 is its Gold support sibling). Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 18).

**Book A3:** *Essential Mathematics for the Victorian Curriculum Year 8, Third Edition* — Australia, Greenwood et al., Cambridge University Press, ©2024, ISBN 978-1-009-48075-8; 24,871,876 bytes, 955 PDF pp. The Victorian Curriculum state edition of the Essential Mathematics Year 8 line — a distinct work from Book A2. Pages cited are printed pages (printed = PDF − 20). Its contents lists Chapter 11, Algorithmic thinking, as “(online only)”, but the full chapter IS bound into this file, after the Answers (printed pp. 926–941), and is read and placed (flag F1).

(1) The staged record files this book as the mainstream Azerbaijani-sector Grade 2 textbook; it is not — it is the special-schools simplified-programme edition, **Part 2 only**, covering numbers 13–20; simplified Part 1 (76 pp) was never staged and is not in hand, and the mainstream Grade 2 *Riyaziyyat* (portal ids 664/665; older whole-book uploads of 147/147/162 pp, all checked and ruled out by page count) is not in hand at all. (2) The file is NOT pusher-secured: phase3-push-state.json lists it as pushed and reconcile sweep #6 (2026-11-05) called it pusher-secured, but a full enumeration of all 9 releases of tensae-code/liqlearns-textbooks (~7,300 assets, via the authenticated GitHub API, 2026-11-10) found NO Azerbaijan Mathematics asset anywhere — the only Azerbaijan assets are Informatics G2 and Technology G2 (mid-size-books-2) and Biology G11 (mid-size-books-3); the book was read from its ministry-repository original (trims.edu.az), identified exactly by its staged record (107 pp, the title-page simplified-programme wording quoted in books.json, and the legacy font encoding). ## Cabo Verde added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Cabo Verde’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **3 books, all of them student (pupil) manuals of the Ministério da Educação de Cabo Verde: *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (Edição 2019), *Manual de Matemática 5º ano* (Edição 2020) and *Matemática 7º ano — Manual do(a) aluno(a)* (Edição 2020); 0 teacher guides** — and placed 197 teaching items (Grade 3: 47, Grade 5: 69, Grade 7: 81; 391 PDF pages, every page read). The expansion map’s count of 3 student + 0 teacher is CORRECT. Set boundary, proved from the records: `textbooks.tsv` carries exactly 17 Cabo Verde rows (all Government, grades 1–7), of which exactly three are Mathematics, all student; the download list’s §C enumerates 93 ministry manuals, including Maths manuals for grades 1, 2, 4, 6, 8, 9, 10 and 11 and applied-Maths manuals — every one of those is exact-link-only (no TSV row, no staging file, no GitHub copy) and NOT in hand, so none is cited here; counting any of them would be a set error. Record gap, reported not fixed (record-side): `phase3-push-state.json` contains no Cabo Verde entry at all, and there is no Cabo Verde folder or `books.json` in phase3-staging — the in-hand evidence is the TSV’s GitHub URLs themselves, and all three GitHub copies were located, verified (byte size and MD5; the Grade 5 ministry copy is byte-identical to the GitHub copy, so that book is one book, cited once) and read directly, so the set is settled despite the push-state gap. No duplicates exist in the set: three distinct grades, three distinct books. Printed page = PDF page in all three books. The placements’ named exclusions — front matter, unit openers, objectives lists and the closing national-anthem page in the Grade 5 and Grade 7 books; the Grade 3 book’s cover plate and welcome/mascot pages (PDF pp. 1, 3, 5), its part-opener pages (PDF pp. 55, 85, 107, 111), its closing test “Teste” (printed pp. 125–129 — mixed assessment re-using taught content, no new teaching) and the flag / national-anthem page (PDF p. 130) — and pure exercise / consolidation blocks that teach nothing new are stocked nowhere. The 12 book errors found in the read (2 in the Grade 5 book, 5 in the Grade 7 book, 5 in the Grade 3 book) are carried corrected on the rows they touch. Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items from Book 1 and Book 2.

**Book 1:** *Manual de Matemática 3º ano* (student manual) — Cabo Verde, Ministério da Educação, Edição 2019; authors Jimmy Leppãnen, Sara Hoye, FvZ; editor Gamlin & Zeipel Communication AB; 140,731,505 bytes, 130 PDF pp., MD5 112ca4f2fc6577b4eb035b0237d9253c (read from the GitHub release copy in release `full-quality-oversized-books`; its byte size equals the ministry HEAD size recorded in the download list §C row 13 exactly). Printed page = PDF page. Pupil textbook.

Printed page = PDF page throughout the body (its unit-opener pages, PDF pp. 6–7, are unnumbered and are cited by PDF page). Pupil textbook.

## Costa Rica added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Costa Rica's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **25 books, all of them student books: the five CONED distance-secondary antologías *Antología de Matemáticas*, Niveles 7–11 (UNED/CONED — Nivel Sétimo 2017, Nivel Noveno 2018, Nivel 11 2019; Nivel Décimo by MSc. Jorge Díaz Porras) and the ITCR Proyecto EVEPRIM primary books for Grades 3 and 6 (10 textos + 10 folletos de prácticas across Relaciones y Álgebra, Números, Geometría, Medidas, Estadística and Probabilidad); 0 teacher guides for Costa Rica Maths exist in hand** — and placed 229 teaching items. **SET CORRECTION: the expansion map's count of 17 student is WRONG** — 17 is exactly the number of Costa Rica Mathematics rows in textbooks.tsv (10 EVEPRIM textos + only 2 of the 10 EVEPRIM folletos + 5 CONED antologías); the TSV was never updated for 8 folletos staged on 2026-10-25, and the staging books.json files and phase3-push-state.json agree on the true set of 25 (the folletos count student-side, per the map's own stated basis). All 25 were grabbed, verified (byte size + MD5 against books.json; the two Relaciones y Álgebra textos, which have no books.json entry, verified by size + title page as the main-repo files) and read in full — no partial excerpts, no duplicates (the SharePoint folder copies of the six texto books matched the staged sizes byte-for-byte, so they are the same files, cited once) and no unopenable files. The three Grade 3 textos were read in their November 2026 editions, the in-hand editions per books.json; the GitHub release mid-size-books-3 still holds the superseded October editions of those three textos (a record-level flag, reported, not fixed — same books, older exports; only the November editions are cited here). Two chapter promises in the CONED books are NOT teaching and place no item anywhere: CONED 7's Capítulo 4 is titled Estadística y Probabilidad but teaches statistics only (probability appears only in its introductory discussion questions, pp. 209–214), and CONED 10's Capítulo 1 objectives promise plane transformations that no Tema in the chapter teaches (only a recall of Grade 8 homothety). The placements' named exclusions — front matter, indices, bibliographies and answer keys (soluciones); the EVEPRIM answer-key chapters and the review chapters that re-teach already-placed sections; the CONED chapter motto/introduction pages and “desafío matemático” pages; and the videos and web interactives the EVEPRIM books link to — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere. The 5 CONED book-error groups found in the read — CONED 7: the angle table (p. 106) labels a 360° angle “CONVEXO”, the inverse-proportionality example (pp. 201–203) states v·t = 247.5 for a product of 330 and “very close to 3 hours” at 78 km/h for ≈4.23 h with garbled fraction work, the triangle inequality (p. 125) omits “any two” sides, the median answer (p. 238) gives 68 for a median of 67, the worked powers page (p. 64) misaligns its results, and a tiling exercise (p. 35) reads “largo por largo”; CONED 8: the table of contents numbers the equations Tema “Tema 4” while the body heads it “Tema 5” (numbering slip only); CONED 9: the SI prefix table (p. 74) gives exa the symbol “R” (exa is E), the radian-to-degree rule (p. 117) is printed inverted/garbled though its worked examples are correct, and the contents duplicates “Función Cuadrática” for Capítulo 3 Temas 1 and 4 and mislabels Capítulo 4 as “Capítulo III”; CONED 10: the falling-body model (p. 373) prints “12” for ½ (the worked values use ½ correctly), the key (p. 42) duplicates “tangentes” where “secantes” is meant, p. 14 asks for the area of a “circunferencia” (the circle), and the pentagon solution (p. 86) drops its degree symbols; CONED 11: the logarithm table (p. 118) misaligns its examples (it prints log₅ 5 = 2, which belongs to log₅ 25), the wound-model solution (p. 172) shows an unsigned division display before the correct result, and “invectiva” (p. 121) is a typo for inyectiva — are carried corrected on the rows they touch. One further note is carried as the placements carry it: the Medidas G3 answer key's building-height estimate reads 45 dam in the October copy and “4 dam” in the November extraction — treated as a re-export/extraction artefact in the key, not teaching content, and cited nowhere. Costa Rica's books are pupil books, so Costa Rica is COUNTED: it joins this lesson's acceptance count wherever the placements stock an item. CONED printed page = PDF page throughout; EVEPRIM pages are cited by PDF page with the book's section numbers (two exceptions print folios: the EVEPRIM 6 Probabilidad texto's printed folio = PDF page − 6 and the EVEPRIM 3 Medidas texto's = PDF page − 7; citations give PDF pages). Into this lesson the placements stock 3 items (items 1, 111 and 115).

**Book 1:** *Antología de Matemáticas — Nivel Sétimo* (student antología) — Costa Rica, Universidad Estatal a Distancia (UNED) / Colegio Nacional de Educación a Distancia (CONED), 2017; 6,973,434 bytes, 242 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page throughout. Pupil book (distance-secondary antología).

**Book 2:** *Relaciones y Álgebra, 3º* (texto) — Costa Rica, Escuela de Matemática, Instituto Tecnológico de Costa Rica (ITCR), Proyecto EVEPRIM 3; ISBN 978-9930-617-51-9; 10,502,672 bytes, 84 PDF pp. The book prints no folio — pages are cited by PDF page with the book's own section numbers (§). Pupil textbook.

**Book 3:** *Relaciones y Álgebra, 3º — Folleto de prácticas* — Costa Rica, Escuela de Matemática, ITCR, Proyecto EVEPRIM 3; 3,084,659 bytes, 21 PDF pp.; a compilation of the practices of the Relaciones y Álgebra texto (its own p. 2). Pages cited by PDF page. Pupil practice book.

Record corrections the read proved, carried exactly: the record's “Matemática 2do grado Parte I student textbook” is in fact the COMPLETE Grade 2 workbook (Cuaderno de trabajo — Matemática 2, ISBN 978-959-13-1854-1) — **no Grade 2 student textbook is in hand**, and Grade 2 rests on its workbook, Programa and Orientaciones; the record's Grade 5 student byte size (36,868,096 B) is a truncated copy — the complete file read is 77,146,019 B / 396 pp; the Grade 11 teacher documents belong to the newer Perfeccionamiento programme and do **not** match the in-hand Grade 11 student book (2005 edition — its limits, derivatives and integrals chapters have no teacher companion in hand, and the programme's trigonometry unit has no chapter in that book), so they are filed as that programme's own teacher evidence, never as evidence for the 2005 book; the MINED portal source URLs now return soft-404 pages — the reads used Wayback captures of those same URLs and the GitHub routes. ## Bulgaria added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Bulgaria's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 files = 4 works, all pupil textbooks: the Бит и Техника (Bit i Tekhnika) *Математика* for Grades 1, 2 and 3 and for Grade 4 in two part-files (1 част / 2 част), 642 PDF pages in all** — and placed 123 teaching items. **The expansion map's “5 student + 0 teacher” is CORRECT as a file count (5 files, 4 works). Full-book vs sample verdict: NO in-hand Maths file is a sample** — every file's extent matches its own imprint's print-sheet count (Grade 1: 20.5 sheets, 128 lessons to printed p. 158 + 12 appendices; Grade 2: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 3: 18 sheets, 112 lessons to printed p. 140; Grade 4: 10 + 11.5 sheets, lessons 1–136), and the 8 proven publisher samples in Bulgaria's library record (Music G6, Foreign Language G6/G7, БЕЛ G10/G11 and Geography G5) contain no Maths book, so the library's sample ruling does not touch this set. All five PDFs are image-only with no usable text layer and were read visually from page renders (cover, imprint, the book's own contents, and body pages for every distinct topic); printed folio = PDF page − 1 in every book, and Grade 4 Part 2 restarts its own pagination (its citations are marked “Part 2”). No Bulgaria Maths book was in the course before this merge — the string “Bulgaria” occurred nowhere under `maths/` — so every placed item is new here. Set boundary: everything else Bulgarian Maths in the records is exact-link / reader only and NOT in hand — Riva Maths G1–G4 PDFs (duplicate slots for the same grades), Klett epub Maths G5/G6 readers, Klett FlipHTML5 Maths G7/G8 (fullness unproven) and Архимед Maths G10/G11 Ourboox PDFs — none is part of this read and none is cited here; if any is later secured and read, Bulgaria reverts to PARTIAL until it is stocked. The placements' named exclusions — each grade's diagnostics, self-checks and consolidation lessons, drills, blitz tournaments, games and projects (other than the Grade 4 data project stocked in the statistics lessons), end-of-unit and year reviews, and the Grade 1 book's 12 cut-out appendices (number cards, shape cut-outs, play money, tangram, clock face, addition wheel, 0–20 ruler) — are apparatus and are stocked nowhere; the topics they practise are counted at the lessons that teach them. **Book-internal flag, carried exactly: the Grade 1 book's introduction (printed p. 4) promises the unit litre, but no lesson teaches it — the contents list no litre lesson and the closing units summary (lesson 127, printed p. 155, read on the body page) lists only hour, euro, eurocent, kilogram and centimetre — so the litre is stocked nowhere, and Bulgaria Grade 1 stands as teaching cm, kg, euro/eurocent and the hour, not the litre.** Book errors proper (wrong mathematics) were found nowhere in the pages read; contents-page slips (e.g. Grade 4 Part 1 listing its angle lessons out of order in the contents) are apparatus slips, not teaching errors, and are counted nowhere. The placements' one NEW LESSON NEEDED topic — calculator use (Grade 4 Part 2, lessons 118–119, item 121) — is stocked in the existing `money-and-time/calculator-use` lesson built by the Sri Lanka expansion merge; no second calculator lesson is built. Into this lesson the placements stock 13 items (items 8, 15, 16, 27, 30, 35, 43, 49, 73, 84, 102, 107, 110 (the addition side of each)).

## Djibouti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Djibouti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 document, the CRIPEN *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher's guide, series « À MOI LES MATHS », édition 2023; 129 pages); 0 pupil (student) textbooks** — and placed 68 teaching items. The document is a teacher's guide: for every lesson of the matching pupil manuel it states the headline objective, the pedagogical intention, the pupil objectives, the lesson conduct, the activities and the worked answers. Every Djibouti item in this section is therefore TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE — the guide teaches teachers how to teach the topic; it is never a pupil textbook — cited as the evidence kind it is; Djibouti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure, on the standing Ghana evidence-only precedent, until a Djibouti pupil book is in hand and read. No pupil Maths book is in hand: the guide states (printed pp. 9–10) that since the 2019 school year teachers and pupils have a matching pupil manuel and livret of the same series (« À MOI LES MATHS », 5ème année), organised in the same four eight-week modules — those pupil books exist but are not in hand (placements record item R1), the guide's references to the manuel's pages are apparatus, not pupil content read here, and the student Maths files the library records locate are link-only partial extracts, never downloaded; none is part of this read and none is cited. Printed page = PDF page throughout the guide. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught content stated in a lesson sheet's headline objective and pupil objectives, splitting a sheet where it teaches two contents that belong to different course lessons; the pedagogical apparatus around each sheet (situation conduct, activity-by-activity commentary, exercise commentaries and synthesis questions) is not re-counted per activity. The placements' named exclusions — the avant-propos, préambule and présentation (printed pp. 3, 8–12: the competency approach, the four “moments of study”, the teacher's role, evaluation and remediation apparatus), the sommaire itself (printed pp. 4–7; its faults are placements record items R2 and R4), the mental-calculation routines (PLM) opening most sheets, per-sheet conduct and commentary counted once per content, and the guide's cross-references to the pupil manuel and livret pages — are stocked nowhere; the one content-bearing statement in the front matter, the end-of-primary objective's domain list (printed p. 9), is placed as a programme item in the ratio-and-proportion lesson. The Module 2 sheet at printed pp. 55–56 is a near-verbatim reprint of the Module 1 sheet at printed pp. 14–15 (placements record item R3); its content is placed once, as item 1. The book error found in the read that touches this lesson is carried corrected on the row it touches: the shopping situation's worked answers on printed pp. 14–15 print 264,100 − 98,000 = 165,600 (correct: 166,100) and a remainder of 39,100 fdj by the addition path; the correct remainder is 500,000 − 463,900 = 36,100 fdj, and the lesson's conclusion (only three articles can be bought) is unaffected (placements error E2). Into this lesson the placements stock 5 teacher-guide-evidence items from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide, Grade 5) — Djibouti, CRIPEN (Ministère de l'Éducation Nationale et de la Formation Professionnelle), édition 2023, series « À MOI LES MATHS »; 1,494,038 bytes, 129 PDF pages. Printed page = PDF page throughout. TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY — the guide teaches the teacher how to teach the topic; it is not a pupil textbook.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Calculating sums and differences of numbers in line or set out in columns, mastering the addition and subtraction techniques (carrying) and solving addition/subtraction situations | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 14–15 |
| Adding the numbers from 0 to 999,999 | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 18–19 |
| Calculating the sum and the difference of large numbers inside problem situations | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 22–23 |
| Adding and subtracting large numbers by the column technique — aligning by rank, grouping digits in threes, not forgetting the carries | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 44–45 |
| Calculating the sum or the difference of large numbers (into the millions) and choosing between addition and subtraction in situations | Djibouti — *Guide de l'enseignant(e) — Mathématiques 5ème année* (teacher guide) — teacher-guide evidence, printed pp. 62–63 |

Djibouti's class for Maths is PARTIAL (teacher-evidence-only): the 1 in-hand book is read in full and stocked, but no pupil textbook is in hand — the matching « À MOI LES MATHS » Grade 5 pupil manuel and livret exist and are named by the guide itself, but no complete pupil Maths file is held, and the located student Maths files are link-only partial extracts, unread.

The files filed as Grade 12 (the student book and the teacher guide) are byte-identical to the Grade 11 files — MINEDUC issues this level as ONE combined 3°–4° Medio volume filed under both grades — so each is cited once, at Grade 11, and the set's headline is 19 distinct books behind 21 filed rows wherever the count is used. All 11 distinct student books are complete full-year books (index, all units and end matter — glossary / answer key / bibliography — verified in place in every volume) and their items are COUNTED normally. **Every held teacher guide is a TOMO 2 partial volume** — covering student Units 3–4 (Grade 4: Units 3–5; the combined 3°–4° Medio guide: the 4° Medio year only, its 3° Medio guidance sitting in the unheld Tomo 1); **no Tomo 1 is held for any grade**, so the guide items below are teacher evidence only, restricted to exactly the units each Tomo 2 contains, named in the rows and never in the acceptance count. ## Finland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Finland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **24 files / 23 distinct works of government-hosted open material (OPH and the aoe.fi Library of Open Educational Resources); Finland has NO national textbook series** — and placed 173 items (51 pupil + 122 teacher evidence). The records' kind labels are wrong for most of the set: 13 of the 18 Mathematics-labelled “student” files are actually teacher-facing (in-service course books, theory texts, activity series and instructor guides), and only 6 files are pupil-facing (the Metsämatikkaa cards, the quadrilateral chart, the weather game, the Päivänselvää chapter, the programming workbook and its model solutions) — every book below is carried at its TRUE kind, never the record label. One byte-identical duplicate (aoe.2674-alku = aoe.2686-alku, the same file under two aoe records) is cited once; the Päivänselvää book is a Chapter-2-only excerpt, cited only for what it holds; the quadrilateral chart's filed copy is 1 content page + 175 blank padding pages. The placements' named exclusions — the weather game's non-temperature content, the computing-pedagogy matter in the two Turku course books, the motivation and mindset material in the JoMa books, the Pikkumatikoita parts 2, 8 and 11, and the programming books' pure-programming tasks — are stocked nowhere. Pupil items are COUNTED normally; teacher-evidence items are named in the rows and stay outside the acceptance count. The Grade 1 Näppituntuma volume's appendices (printed pp. 37–41: number cards 1–100 and a hundred chart, dot cards, and < = > sign cards) are apparatus for its placed items and are stocked nowhere as content. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 pupil item and 8 teacher-evidence items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3 and Book 4.

## Dominican Republic added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read the Dominican Republic's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **14 books, all of them student books (0 teacher guides exist in hand)**: the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books of MINERD's Proyecto Libro Abierto, Serie 1 (*Matemática* 2°–6° Primaria and *Matemática* 1°–6° Secundaria), plus the mathematics content of the 3 integrated *Primero* volumes (Grade 1 has no dedicated *Matemática* book; only the three volumes' Matemática blocks are placed) — 3,184 PDF pages in all — and placed 661 teaching items (614 from the dedicated books, 47 from the Primero volumes). Every book was grabbed fresh by the authenticated GitHub API route, verified byte-exact against its staging `books.json` byte count and cover/title-proved, and the grabbed copy deleted after reading; the recorded Google Drive fallback is login-walled from this host and delivered no book. The expansion map's “16 student” count is wrong as a count of *Matemática* books: the dedicated set is 11, and 16 is reachable only by adding integrated all-subject files that are not *Matemática* books. Printed page = PDF page in every book (verified on the unit dividers and page footers). The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught topic — a named section or a coherent teaching block inside a unit. The placements' named exclusions — the apparatus of all 14 books (unit-opening, group-activity, evaluation, self-assessment, bibliography and book-care pages, except the five group activities that teach a topic found nowhere else in their book, which are placed); the *Primero Volumen I* Matemática diagnostic evaluation; the Primero competency pages; the Primero volumes' non-maths sequences (with two maths-adjacent blocks inside them named in the placements and stocked nowhere); the 5 Nivel Inicial integrated books (a scope note, not part of the Maths set); and one record artefact that is not a book (a stray partial-upload `.part` file in the grade-04 push state) — are stocked nowhere. One book error is carried corrected on the row it touches: *Matemática 3° Primaria*'s weight table (evaluation, printed p. 152; the same table family at p. 147) pairs 8 lb with 48 oz — wrong: 1 lb = 16 oz, so 8 lb = 128 oz (48 oz is 3 lb). Minor slips, no action: the contents lists of *Matemática* 2° and 3° Secundaria run one page behind the printed folios — every citation uses the folios. Checked and NOT an error: *Matemática 4° Secundaria*, printed p. 84, prints Pythagoras as a² = b² + c² with a defined as the hypotenuse — correct as printed. In the sections below the books are cited as Books 1–14 in the placements' order. The Dominican Republic COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these student-book rows. The Dominican Republic's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Maths book (the 11 dedicated *Matemática* books plus the Primero volumes carrying Grade 1's maths) has been read in full and placed.

Into this lesson the placements stock 11 items from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 12 and Book 13.

**Book 1:** *Matemática 2° Primaria* (student book, Libro Abierto Serie 1) — Dominican Republic, MINERD (Ministerio de Educación), Proyecto Libro Abierto; 71,300,614 bytes, 176 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page.

**Book 2:** *Matemática 3° Primaria* (student book, Libro Abierto Serie 1) — Dominican Republic, MINERD (Ministerio de Educación), Proyecto Libro Abierto; 83,680,456 bytes, 176 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page.

**Book 3:** *Matemática 4° Primaria* (student book, Libro Abierto Serie 1) — Dominican Republic, MINERD (Ministerio de Educación), Proyecto Libro Abierto; 74,141,405 bytes, 208 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page.

**Book 4:** *Matemática 5° Primaria* (student book, Libro Abierto Serie 1) — Dominican Republic, MINERD (Ministerio de Educación), Proyecto Libro Abierto; 66,948,345 bytes, 208 PDF pp. Printed page = PDF page.

## Guyana added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Guyana's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDF — **1 book, a student (pupil) textbook: *Let's Do Mathematics Book 5* (Ministry of Education, Georgetown, Guyana — Easy Path Series), the Grade 5 book; 0 teacher guides** — and placed 140 teaching items. The expansion map's count of 1 student + 0 teacher is CORRECT, proved from five records (textbooks.tsv, phase3-push-state.json, phase3-push-state-lane2.json, the staging books.json files and download-lists/guyana.md): Guyana has 6 books in hand in total and exactly one is Mathematics. The other five in-hand books (G1 *Science Around Us Book 1*, G1 *Social Studies For Our Children Book 1*, G2 *Fun With Language Book 2 Part 1*, G3 *Fun With Language Book 3*, G6 *Science Around Us Book 6*) were each byte-verified against their records and their covers/title pages read — each is the book its record names; **no maths book is hiding under another subject label**. All other Guyana Maths books in the records (Easy Path Books 1–4 and 6, the nursery numeracy workbooks and the “Mathematics Made Easy” series) are link-only — exact URLs recorded, no file in hand — and were not read. The one book was read from staging in place: 5,990,840 bytes (= the books.json record), 169 PDF pages, MD5 c6128b33f8842397e0351776a7f36929, and it is the COMPLETE book, not a partial excerpt — the contents page (PDF p. 7) lists all 30 units, every unit start was verified at its contents page, and the text runs printed pp. 1–158, ending with the final “Let Us Look Back” exercises (printed p. 158) and the back cover. Printed page = PDF page − 8 throughout (Unit 1 begins printed p. 1 = PDF p. 9). Book observation F1, carried exactly: the cover reads “Revised Edition 2004” (the staging books.json and the checklist follow the cover) while the imprint page (PDF p. 2) reads “© 2005 … Revised 2005” — one consistent scan of one book, so the edition is cited as “Revised Edition 2004 (cover); imprint © 2005”. The placements' counting grain is one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The placements' named exclusions — front matter (acknowledgements, foreword, preface, contents; PDF pp. 1–8); every end-of-unit REVIEW section and every “LET US LOOK BACK” evaluative set (after Units 5, 10, 15, 20, 25 and at the end of Unit 30, printed pp. 156–158 — revision and assessment exercises that restate items already placed, no new skill taught); worked story problems and exercise sets inside the units (practice of the item taught on that page, not separate items); and the example exchange rates themselves (dated examples that differ between Units 20 and 21 by design; the taught skill stocked is the conversion method) — are stocked nowhere. Guyana COUNTS in a lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of these pupil-book rows. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item from Book 1 (item 21).

**Book 1:** *Let's Do Mathematics Book 5* (student book, Grade 5, Easy Path Series) — Guyana, Ministry of Education, Georgetown; Revised Edition 2004 (cover); imprint © 2005 / Revised 2005; 5,990,840 bytes, 169 PDF pp., MD5 c6128b33f8842397e0351776a7f36929 (read from staging in place). Printed page = PDF page − 8. Pupil textbook.

## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon stocks only evidence rows in this lesson — teacher-evidence and curriculum-requirement, with no pupil-textbook row — so it is named in the countries line and in the rows but is NOT added to this lesson's acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 15 items (14 teacher-evidence, 1 curriculum-requirement) from Book 1, Book 2, Book 3, Book 4, Book 5, Book 6.

**Book 1:** *Curriculum Mathématiques 2e année* (curriculum document — programme officiel) — Gabon, Institut Pédagogique National (IPN); 1,526,493 bytes, 50 PDF pp. A curriculum states what must be taught; it teaches nothing itself — its rows are curriculum-requirement evidence, never counted as pupil textbooks. Pages cited are the PDF pages; each requirement is cited by compétence and palier, as the placements give it.

## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 20 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 1*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 2*), Book 3 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 4 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 5 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 6 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 7 (*Year 7 Mathematics Textbook*).

Printed page = PDF page − 2. Scan defect: printed p. 57 appears twice (PDF pp. 59–60 are the same leaf) and printed p. 58 is absent — the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand and is cited nowhere.

Printed page = PDF page − 3 for the in-sequence body; the scan order is scrambled at the end and the capacity-discovery activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51), cited once.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 items — 4 pupil-textbook rows and 4 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado*), Book 2 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 4to grado*), Book 7 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 1er grado*), Book 8 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas and 2do grado*).
