## Vanuatu added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Vanuatu's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — exactly 1 book, a teacher guide, and no pupil textbook: *Tija Gaed blong Matematiks Yia 3* (Mathematics Teacher Guide, Year 3, Bislama, MoET 2017). The expansion map is CORRECT for the in-hand set (0 student + 1 teacher = 1); the TSV's other distinct Vanuatu books are the Language & Communication Y2, Living in Community Y2 and Science Y3 teacher guides and the Bislama Spelling Dictionary Y2 — no Maths hides under another subject label and there is no "all subjects" Vanuatu volume in hand. The staging PDF was deleted after pushing (the pusher's normal pattern); the read copy was grabbed from the recorded source via its banked Wayback capture (2019-11-17 `id_` replay of education.gov.vu/docs/textbooks/Matematiks_2017.pdf) and is byte-identical to the record: 11,818,504 bytes, 317 PDF pages, SHA-256 2b7aa33d2ee0a81d9512e4f141c208bfc04a0abdf6d5bf77d58356a31cf22ec6, matching the staging `books.json` hash exactly. Printed folio = PDF page \u2212 1 throughout (verified on the outcomes section, weekly lesson footers and the glossary). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching topic in the Year 3 column of the guide's Learning Outcomes & Indicators tables (printed pp. 12\u201322), worded from the outcome and its indicators and confirmed against the weekly lessons that teach it (Terms 1\u20133, printed pp. 23\u2013294); items taught in a weekly lesson but stated in no Year 3 outcome are marked (D). Assessment questions, resources lists and vocabulary boxes are apparatus for the counted items, not separate items. Record items R1\u2013R4 are stocked nowhere as teaching items: R1 the trilingual Bislama\u2013English\u2013French glossary of Year 3 mathematics words (printed pp. 313\u2013316) — teacher-reference apparatus; R2 the photocopiable appendix masters (printed pp. 295\u2013312) — they carry no teaching topic not already counted; R3 the programme facts from the introduction (printed pp. 4\u20136 — five strands, ten sub-strands, seven hours of Year 3 mathematics a week under the National Timetable Policy 2013); R4 the Beginning / Year 1 / Year 2 columns of the outcomes tables — those are the earlier years' guides, which are NOT in hand for Maths, and nothing from them is counted. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the introduction apart from R3 (printed pp. 4\u201310 — lesson planning, literacy and inclusive-education guidance, teaching craft only); the Beginning/Year 1/Year 2 progression columns; the weekly vocabulary boxes, resources lists and assessment apparatus; and syllabus/timetable exposition carried as R3 only. Book errors E1\u2013E5 are carried taught corrected where they affect stocking: E1 — printed p. 15 abbreviates millilitres "(mm)" (mm is millimetres; millilitres are mL) and writes litres "(l)"; E2 — the Term 3 Week 3 length-conversion lesson (printed p. 233) is headed with the operations outcome NO.3.V1 though it is measurement; E3 — the money lesson (printed p. 202) cites its outcome as "M3.V1" for MM.3.V1; E4 — the Year 3 probability outcome code is printed "CPD.3.V1" (printed p. 21) against the CDP codes of the other columns; E5 — template slips in the term overviews (printed pp. 23, 210), none affecting content. Set boundary: the ministry `/docs/curriculum/` Maths Student's Books Y3\u2013Y6 (English stream), the French-stream Maths 3e\u20136e Ann\u00e9e, the Mathematics Teacher's Guide (Year 4) and the Kindergarten Guide (2013) are exact-URL candidates only — never downloaded, in no `books.json`, not in the push-state, not in hand and not read. Vanuatu is **TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide teaches, Vanuatu is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Vanuatu's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 teacher-evidence item(s) from Book 1.

Teacher guide \u2014 TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed folio = PDF page \u2212 1.
